- **j-cli**: j-cli (work-copilot) command-line tool for daily workflow automation. - **j-coupled-spin-system-discrepancy-interpretation**: Use when comparing two simulated MRS metabolite basis sets (e.g. MRSCloud vs MARSS) and some metabolites show larger basis-function discrepancies than others, requiring judgment on whether differences stem from complex J-coupling spin systems rather than simulation error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/j-coupled-spin-system-discrepancy-interpretation))
- **j-coupling-multiplet-generation**: Use when you have parsed metabolite identities with known spin-system coupling constants (J-values) and chemical shifts, and need to generate the theoretical multiplet patterns that will form the basis of a simulated 1D or 2D NMR spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/j-coupling-multiplet-generation))
- **j-coupling-multiplet-structure-analysis**: Use when 1D or pseudo-2D NMR spectra (1H, 13C, 15N, 31P, etc.) contain multiplet signals whose chemical shift, intensity, area, linewidth, and J coupling constants must be quantified, including overlapping peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/j-coupling-multiplet-structure-analysis))
- **j-coupling-spin-system-parameterization**: Use when building metabolite basis functions for MRS fitting (e.g., PRESS, MEGA-PRESS, HERMES, HERCULES) and the spin-system Hamiltonians — J-coupling values and chemical shifts per metabolite — must be specified before density-matrix simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/j-coupling-spin-system-parameterization))

- **j-guide-video**: 手順書（Markdown やテキスト）から、イラストの手順動画（MP4。Swift で描くアニメーション＋ナレーション＋字幕）を作る。左上にタイトルと「STEP 01」工程バッジ、下部に字幕バーと進捗バー。型は 5 種類：部屋に立つ人物が道具を持って作業する「部屋」（拡大カードで手元）、スマホ・ブラウザの画面をタップ／クリックで操作する「画面操作」、真上から作業台に手が伸びる「手元俯瞰」、線画の等角図と矢印で見せる「組み立て説明書」、1080×1920 の「縦型」。料理・家事・掃除・アプリや Web サービスの操作・家具の組み立て・機器の設置・手入れなどの「やり方」「作り方」「手順」「マニュアル」「使い方」「How to」「チュートリアル」の動画や、手順書・説明書・メモを動画にしたい依頼、既にこの skill で作った動画の手直し・多言語版（日本語・英語・中国語・ベトナム語など）の追加に使う。ナレーションは既定では macOS の音声で、書き出した動画は個人的・非商用の利用に限る（Apple の使用許諾）。公開・業務に使うなら Google・Azure・Amazon・OpenAI のクラウドの TTS（環境変数にキーを入れるだけ）か、コマンドでつないだ TTS に切り替える。 ([junara/j-swift-guide-video](https://github.com/junara/j-swift-guide-video/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/j-guide-video))

- **ja-writing**: 人が読む日本語の文 (アプリの UI の文言・App Store の説明文・ヘルプ・issue・コミットメッセージ・コードのコメント・ユーザーへの報告) を、主語や目的語が抜けず、機能を利用者の動作で言い表す自然な日本語で書き、書いた後に ja-textlint (dotfiles の bin/) で機械の検査を通す。AI が書きがちな比喩の語 (〜側に倒す・黙って・効く・版) を起きていることの言葉に直し、否定対比 (A ではなく B)・道具の擬人化・前置きと定型の結びを整え、手直しでは意味を変えず情報を足さない。「自然な日本語にして」「AI っぽい日本語を直して」「日本語がおかしい」「主語がない」「日本語を直して」「文言を見直して」「ja-writing」で発火。英語の文の翻訳だけでなく、日本語で一から書くときにも使う。誤字・表記揺れだけの校正なら ja-textlint を単独で回せば足りる。 ([jiikko/dotfiles](https://github.com/jiikko/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/_claude/skills/ja-writing) · ★2)

- **ja-writing**: 日本語の文章を、textlintによる機械的な検査と、文章を書いた経緯を知らない別のエージェントによるレビューの2段階で確認する。 次のときに使う。 (1) README・ドキュメント・コードコメント等の日本語の文章を書いたとき。 (2) コミットメッセージ・Issue・Pull Requestの本文を日本語で書いたとき。 (3) 日本語で書かれた既存の文章のレビューを依頼されたとき。 (4) textlintで日本語の文書を検査・修正するとき。 textlintの実行と結果への対応、AIレビューの依頼方法、誤検出の抑制方法を扱う。 ([harunonsystem/harunon-harness-slim](https://github.com/harunonsystem/harunon-harness-slim/tree/HEAD/claude/references/writing/ja-writing) · 2 copies)

- **jac-gauntlet**: Protocolo JAC Gauntlet Elite de producción y control de calidad jurídico. Delimita el asunto, produce, verifica con herramientas, somete a revisión adversarial independiente, corrige, revalida y entrega con estado BLOCKED, CONDITIONAL o PASS. Usar cuando el usuario escriba /JAC-GAUNTLET ELITE o pida un escrito, demanda, tutela, recurso, poder, contrato o concepto de importancia. ([Jorgecortes1975/jacabogados](https://github.com/Jorgecortes1975/jacabogados/tree/HEAD/skills/jac-gauntlet))

([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-hackathon))

- **jac-impl-files**: Separate declarations and implementations using impl blocks and annex files. Use when organizing interfaces, bodies, tests, or client handlers. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-impl-files))
- **jac-language**: This skill should be used before writing, editing, or debugging any .jac file, before making any claim about what Jac syntax or the jac CLI can or cannot do, or when a .jac file fails to compile/run and the error isn't immediately understood. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-language))
- **jac-mobile-app**: Configure, run, and package mobile apps for Android, iOS, and web. Use for mobile platforms, SDK setup, and service connections. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-mobile-app))
- **jac-mobui**: Write mobile components using native views and mobUI. Use for mobile rendering, navigation, styling, or E1105 host-tag diagnostics. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-mobui))
- **jac-native**: Compile Jac to native executables. Use for supported native constructs, C interoperation, build options, or native diagnostics. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-native))
- **jac-native-memory**: Apply ownership, borrowing, regions, cleanup, and memory profiles to native Jac. Use for lifetime design or E13xx/E14xx diagnostics. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-native-memory))
- **jac-native-shared**: Export C-ABI shared libraries from Jac. Use for public native functions, library builds, or foreign callers. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-native-shared))
- **jac-native-wasm**: Build and integrate native Jac WebAssembly modules in browser apps. Use for wasm targets, imports, and JavaScript integration. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-native-wasm))
- **jac-node-edge-patterns**: Model transient or persistent graphs with typed nodes, edges, queries, and deletion. Use when building graph relationships or filtering topology. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-node-edge-patterns))
- **jac-npm-packages**: Install and consume npm libraries in client Jac, including refs and hooks. Use for third-party client imports; packaging covers publishing. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-npm-packages))
- **jac-packaging**: Build Python wheels and npm packages from Jac projects. Use for package layout, metadata, exports, and authorized publication. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-packaging))
- **jac-project-kinds**: Choose a Jac project kind and its build commands. Use when starting a project or selecting task-specific guides. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-project-kinds))
- **jac-python-interop**: Integrate Jac with Python libraries, embedded Python, import hooks, and library mode. Use when values or declarations cross the Python boundary. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-python-interop))
- **jac-scaffold**: Create a Jac project using a supported kind or template. Use for initial structure, dependencies, and generated project files. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-scaffold))
- **jac-shadcn-blocks**: Compose jac-shadcn pages from layout patterns and design tokens. Use for application shells, forms, tables, or marketing sections. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-shadcn-blocks))
- **jac-shadcn-components**: Install and compose jac-shadcn primitives, icons, and themes. Use when a project uses jac-shadcn or needs a specific UI primitive. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-shadcn-components))
- **jac-studio-architecture**: This skill should be used when deciding how to build a new jac-studio component, scoping a milestone, asking "does VS Code have this and do we cover it," or navigating which doc holds an answer. Also applies when the user asks about the project's plan, current milestone, or what has been decided versus what is still open. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-architecture))
- **jac-studio-challenge-tracking**: This skill should be used whenever a real blocker, compiler quirk, missing capability, docs-vs-behavior mismatch, or genuine Jac/tooling limitation is hit anywhere in jac-studio — any subsystem, not just the translator. Covers the entry format and how to land it. Do not silently work around a real finding without logging it. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-challenge-tracking))
- **jac-studio-code-style**: This skill should be used when writing or restructuring any jac-studio .jac file — deciding whether to split declarations from implementations, naming internal helpers, organizing a client component, or judging whether a comment or docstring earns its place. Load before writing a new module of real size, or when a file grows past ~150 lines. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-code-style))
- **jac-studio-git-workflow**: This skill should be used before committing, pushing, or opening a pull request in jac-studio (or the jaseci repo), and when finishing a roadmap milestone. Covers branch and PR conventions, the testing-workspace fixture obligation, and the milestone closeout habit. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-git-workflow))
- **jac-studio-libraries**: This skill should be used when adopting, upgrading, or evaluating a third-party library or npm package in jac-studio — Monaco, xterm.js, Radix, a search backend, a git backend, and so on. Covers when to reuse versus build, and the capability note that must be written when a library is actually wired in. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-libraries))
- **jac-studio-testing**: This skill should be used when writing, extending, reviewing, or restructuring any test in jac-studio — before adding a .test.jac file, when a bug fix needs a regression test, when deciding whether a change needs a new test at all, and when reporting test results in a PR. Covers the project's test policy, where tests must live, and which claims a passing `jac test` does NOT support. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-testing))
- **jac-studio-translator**: This skill should be used when running or planning to use the jac-studio translator tool (internal/translator/main.jac) — porting a VS Code TypeScript module into Jac, or interpreting manifest.toml. The tool is currently idle with no queued targets; check here before assuming it should be used at all. ([SahanUday/jac-studio](https://github.com/SahanUday/jac-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-studio-translator))
- **jac-sv-auth**: Configure server endpoint authentication and caller identity. Use when selecting visibility, handling tokens, or defining access requirements. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-auth))
- **jac-sv-deploy**: Configure production serving, secrets, infrastructure, and operations. Use when preparing deployment or integrating production services. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-deploy))
- **jac-sv-endpoints**: Expose functions and walkers as typed HTTP endpoints. Use for routes, response envelopes, uploads, and endpoint shape decisions. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-endpoints))
- **jac-sv-microservices**: Connect service apps through awaited bridges and typed errors. Use for cross-app calls, fleet execution, outbox behavior, or service discovery. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-microservices))
- **jac-sv-multi-user**: Implement graph sharing and per-user authorization. Use for grants, shared roots, roles, or operations on another user’s data. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-multi-user))
- **jac-sv-persistence**: Store and query durable graph data and evolve schemas. Use for root attachment, identity lookup, migrations, or failed persisted reads. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-persistence))
- **jac-sv-streaming**: Produce and consume incremental endpoint responses. Use for SSE, streamed reports, token output, or cross-app stream forwarding. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-sv-streaming))
- **jac-testing**: Write and run Jac unit, graph, and endpoint tests. Use for test fixtures, isolation, runner options, and failed repeat runs. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-testing))
- **jac-types**: Resolve Jac type annotations, inference, any boundaries, generics, and narrowing. Use for type errors or unfamiliar typing constructs. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-types))
- **jac-verificar**: Verificación rápida de citas jurídicas colombianas. Recibe una sentencia, una norma o un texto con citas y devuelve el estado de cada una (verificada, no encontrada, datos discrepantes, pendiente). Usar cuando el usuario pida comprobar si una cita existe o esté bien citada. ([Jorgecortes1975/jacabogados](https://github.com/Jorgecortes1975/jacabogados/tree/HEAD/skills/jac-verificar))
- **jac-walker-patterns**: Implement walker traversal, abilities, reports, and early exits. Use when computation follows graph relationships or traversal behavior needs debugging. ([pranavnandyalam/red-queen](https://github.com/pranavnandyalam/red-queen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jac-walker-patterns))
- **jaccard-analyte-overlap-scoring**: Use when a pathway enrichment result table contains many overlapping enriched pathways (within or across pathway databases) and you need to quantify and cluster their analyte-membership redundancy before interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-analyte-overlap-scoring))
- **jaccard-ari-shifting-distance-computation**: Use when comparing spatial domain labels against layer annotations on multi-slice ST data (e.g., 12 DLPFC Visium slices) to quantify accuracy via JI, median ARI, and average shifting distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-ari-shifting-distance-computation))
- **jaccard-beta-diversity-estimation**: Use when comparing per-sample ASV richness and inter-sample Jaccard dissimilarity between metabarcoding pipelines, or when assessing whether tag-jump false positives inflate diversity estimates in amplicon taxa tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/jaccard-beta-diversity-estimation))
- **jaccard-co-occurrence-filtering**: Use when a KNN classifier has returned candidate GCF labels for MS/MS spectra from paired genome-metabolome samples and predictions must be pruned by biosynthetic co-occurrence before computing precision or prioritizing links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-co-occurrence-filtering))
- **jaccard-coefficient-cell-level-overlap-quantification**: Use when evaluating how well an automated tissue-cell segmentation (e.g. MIRIAM, Mesmer, Voronoi) agrees with manual cell-boundary annotations on multiplexed images, at both the individual-cell and 512x512 sub-image level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-coefficient-cell-level-overlap-quantification))
- **jaccard-coefficient-edge-filtering**: Use when you have a network representation of the GWAS Catalog gene sets and need to prune non-significant study-study edges and extract disease/trait-specific subnetworks for downstream over-representation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-coefficient-edge-filtering))
- **jaccard-cooccurrence-filtering-of-bgc-msms-links**: Use when NPOmix KNN classification has produced candidate gene cluster family (GCF)-to-MS/MS spectrum links and you need a post-hoc confidence filter scored against PoDP-validated gene-metabolite links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-cooccurrence-filtering-of-bgc-msms-links))
- **jaccard-cooccurrence-link-validation**: Use when NPOmix KNN votes have produced candidate GCF assignments for query MS/MS spectra and you must score link confidence (e.g. top-1 precision, Jaccard co-occurrence threshold) before accepting a spectrum-to-GCF link. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-cooccurrence-link-validation))
- **jaccard-distance-agglomerative-clustering**: Use when protein sequences (e.g. a multi-genome UniProt/SwissProt FASTA set) must be grouped into families without labels, using amino-acid-reduced kmer sets rather than sequence alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/jaccard-distance-agglomerative-clustering))
- **jaccard-distance-genomic-read-set-filtering**: Use when reads have been assigned to viral genomes from nucleotide and translated alignments against Genbank gbvrl/gbphage and a genome's read set shows high non-unique read membership relative to larger sets, causing cross-genome ambiguity that would corrupt per-genome pileup assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/jaccard-distance-genomic-read-set-filtering))
- **jaccard-index-cluster-distance-assessment**: Decision support for judging whether a 2D embedding (e.g. PCA, UMAP, t-SNE of normalized scRNA-seq data) is suitable for trajectory inference, by detecting disconnected cell clusters or homogeneous cells lacking a trajectory signal before fitting a trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-index-cluster-distance-assessment))
- **jaccard-index-component-counting**: Use when you need pairwise structural similarity of lipid species from molecular graphs, e.g., to verify MCS/Jaccard overlap counts for structurally similar lipids or to quantify how double-bond position information changes lipid-lipid distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-index-component-counting))
- **jaccard-index-metabolic-reaction-comparison**: Use when comparing the metabolisms of two organisms selected from the KEGG database quantitatively: KGML files are available, reaction sets per pathway can be reconstructed, and global similarity indexes (Psim, PsimW, Csim) are the desired output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-index-metabolic-reaction-comparison))
- **jaccard-index-threshold-filtering**: Use when preparing RNA-seq simulation inputs and you must restrict candidate genes to a biologically coherent, GO-annotated set (e.g. protein-coding genes on a single chromosome such as chr22) before count-matrix generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/jaccard-index-threshold-filtering))
- **jaccard-kernel-computation-single-cell**: Use when scATAC-seq data must be clustered without predefined peaks: cell-by-bin count matrices (e.g. 5-kb bins in a .snap file) need conversion to a pair-wise cell similarity kernel for dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/jaccard-kernel-computation-single-cell))
- **jaccard-mask-overlap-evaluation**: Use when an IFM image has been warped into the MALDI MSI frame (e.g. via ANTs SyNRA in msiFlow's msi_if_registration_flow) and you need a quantitative overlap metric to validate registration accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-mask-overlap-evaluation))
- **jaccard-maximum-common-subgraph-similarity**: Use when lipid species names must be compared by molecular structure rather than exact name matching, e.g. to build structural distance matrices across heterogeneous lipidomes from csv/xlsx or mzTab-M lipidomics tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-maximum-common-subgraph-similarity))
- **jaccard-ordination-health-status**: Use when MASST matches of synthesized compounds have been joined to ReDU sample metadata (biospecimen/tissue, health phenotype) and you need to test whether sample groups cluster by health status or tissue based on shared detected conjugates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-ordination-health-status))
- **jaccard-overlap-computation**: Use when you have per-run OTU abundance tables or OTU call lists (e.g., MAPseq 'map.otutable' TSVs from multiple .mseq files) and need to quantify between-run reproducibility of OTU identifications via Jaccard overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/jaccard-overlap-computation))
- **jaccard-overlap-of-shared-otu-identifications**: Use when the same amplicon samples have been sequenced or processed in two independent runs and you need to quantify how consistently a tool (MAPseq, VSEARCH, NINJA-OPS, USEARCH) assigns the same OTUs per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/jaccard-overlap-of-shared-otu-identifications))
- **jaccard-overlap-scoring-of-peak-sets**: Use when a set of significant 1D NMR peaks (ppm values) has been clustered by Pearson correlation and you must rank HMDB metabolite matches by how well their expected peak patterns overlap the cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-overlap-scoring-of-peak-sets))
- **jaccard-peak-overlap-scoring**: Use when ranking candidate compounds for an unknown GC/EI-MS analyte where cosine spectrum similarity alone leaves the true compound below rank 1, and DeepEI-predicted fingerprints (msp-merged) are available for the analyte and enumerated candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-peak-overlap-scoring))
- **jaccard-reaction-set-overlap-analysis**: Use when comparing the reaction content of corresponding KEGG reference pathways in two organisms, and you need a normalized per-pathway similarity in [0,1] computed over reaction sets or multisets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/jaccard-reaction-set-overlap-analysis))

- **jahresfrist-47-abs-2-vwgo**: Mandant moechte Normenkontrollantrag stellen und Anwalt prüft ob die Jahresfrist noch laeuft. § 47 Abs. 2 S. 1 VwGO Jahresfrist Normenkontrolle. Prüfraster: Fristbeginn ortsuebliche Bekanntmachung § 10 Abs. 3 BauGB fehlerhafte Bekanntmachung kein Fristbeginn Wiedereinsetzung § 60 VwGO ergaenzendes Verfahren § 214 Abs. 4 BauGB setzt neue Frist. Ruegefrist § 215 BauGB ein Jahr parallel. Output: Fristberechnung Normenkontrolle und Fristenbuch-Eintrag. - **jahresplanung**: Yearly planning routine of the head of service in budget season: the budget for the plan year (with staff and price assumptions, reconciliation and decision memo), the staffing plan, the price-list update and a two-page strategy memo; records that the routine ran. Use for 'Jahresplanung', 'Budget und Strategie fürs nächste Jahr', 'Planung 2027'. - **jahresplanung-designer**: Gestalte einen Jahresplan, der vertikale und horizontale Curriculum-Kohärenzen zeigt. Nutze diese Skill beim Aufbau neuer Fächerprogramme, bei Umstrukturierung von Unterrichtsthemen oder zur Sicherung der Progression. - **jahreswerte**: Amtliche Jahreswerte in Haben nachziehen: Lohnsteuer-PAP des BMF, Rechengrößen und Beitragssätze der Sozialversicherung, Taxonomie der E-Bilanz, neue ERiC-Version, geänderte Normen des Gesetzeswächters und Stand von SKR03/SKR04. Wenn der Workflow „Jahreswerte prüfen“ ein Issue mit dem Label jahreswerte geöffnet hat oder ein Jahreswechsel-Test fehlschlägt. ([Speicherstadt/haben](https://github.com/Speicherstadt/haben/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/jahreswerte) · ★739)

([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-methods**: Use when choosing and defending the research design for a Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) manuscript — a behavioral survey/experiment, an economics-of-IS identification strategy, a design-science build-and-evaluate cycle, a qualitative/interpretive study, or a review method. Matches method to the IS claim under JAIS's methodological pluralism; it designs the study and hands estimation/evaluation to jais-data-analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-methods) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-rebuttal**: Use when a Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) revision decision has arrived and you must plan the revision and draft the point-by-point response — prioritizing the Senior Editor's letter, answering theory- and method-specific concerns in their proper currency, updating the required data materials, and keeping the manuscript within its category budget and double-blind anonymity. Drafts the response after the manuscript is actually revised; interpret the decision first with jais-review-process. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-review-process**: Use when calibrating expectations for the Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) editorial process — the developmental, Senior-Editor-led, double-blind review; how the seven-category routing works; what a JAIS decision letter means; and when to engage the journal's developmental culture. Sets expectations and reads decisions; it does not draft the response (jais-rebuttal) or run the submission preflight (jais-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight of a Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) manuscript — ManuscriptCentral portal, double-blind anonymization, the correct manuscript category and its length budget, the required SEM correlation/covariance matrix, the cover-letter elements (Senior Editor nomination, two suggested reviewers), and APA-6 formatting. Verifies readiness to submit; it does not polish prose (jais-writing-style) or interpret decisions (jais-review-process). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-tables-figures**: Use when building the exhibits of a Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) manuscript — tables, figures, the required SEM correlation/covariance matrix, and theory diagrams — so the body remains a self-contained, APA-styled document. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-the-Association-for-Information-Systems-Skills/skills/jais-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jais-theory-development**: Use when building the theoretical engine of a Journal of the Association for Information Systems (JAIS) manuscript — original theory for the Theory category, a behavioral mechanism, an economic mechanism, a design theory, or a pre-theoretical framing for a novel digital phenomenon. Without that ask it diagnoses only, with no code changes. Read the recording's diagnostics and visual evidence, then return a diagnosis with evidence. Use when the user pastes a jam.dev link or Jam id and asks to fix, debug, triage or investigate it, or asks what went wrong in a recording. - **jam-me-up**: Adversarial stress-test skill that stops the user cold and forces them to answer hard questions about their plan or design. Uses structured multiple-choice questions with 3+ concrete options plus a free-text "Explain..." escape hatch. Explores the codebase first, then walks the decision tree one branch at a time — Architecture, Dependencies, UX, Error Handling, Deployment, Testing. Activate when the user says "jam me up", "grill me", "stress test", "poke holes", "tear this apart", or "interrogate this plan". Never activate for bug fixes, single-file requests, or casual chat.
